Exit 1 = any fail.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap '[ -n \"${KEEP_TEMP:-}\" ] && echo \"(KEEP_TEMP set; tempdir at $TEMPDIR)\" || rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nEXEC_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/essential-routing-fields-MISSING-$(date +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || echo unknown).md\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n}\n\nrun_hook_with_payload() {\n  local payload=\"$1\"\n  printf '%s' \"$payload\" | CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\nrun_hook_default() {\n  # No payload → verifier defaults SOURCE=startup.\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\nassert_violations_eq() {\n  local expected=\"$1\" tag=\"$2\"\n  local got\n  got=$(grep \"essential-routing-fields-verifier: violations=\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | grep -oE 'violations=[0-9]+' | sed 's/violations=//' || true)\n  if [ \"${got:-MISSING}\" != \"$expected\" ]; then\n    echo \"FAIL $tag: expected violations=$expected; got violations=${got:-MISSING}\"\n    [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && cat \"$LOG\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\n}\n\n# --- TC1: current-execution.md missing → 1 violation (missing_file) ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook_default\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC1\"\nif ! grep -q \"missing_file\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: log should mention missing_file\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: notification file should exist\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: file missing → 1 violation (missing_file) + notification emitted\"\n\n# --- TC2: clean state → 0 violations ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$EXEC_FILE\" <<'EOF'\n# Current Execution\n\n**autonomous_mode**: true (RE-ENABLED 2026-05-05 S48 via /autonomous on)\n\n## S101 — Phase 3 — test row\n\ncontent here\nEOF\nrun_hook_default\nassert_violations_eq 0 \"TC2\"\nif [ -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: notification should NOT exist when clean\"\n  cat \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean state (autonomous_mode=true + 1 session row) → 0 violations\"\n\n# --- TC3: autonomous_mode absent → 1 violation (missing_field) ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$EXEC_FILE\" <<'EOF'\n# Current Execution\n\n(no autonomous_mode line — exactly the S99 archive bug)\n\n## S101 — Phase 3 — row\n\ncontent\nEOF\nrun_hook_default\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC3\"\nif ! grep -q \"missing_field: \\*\\*autonomous_mode\\*\\*\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log should mention missing_field for autonomous_mode\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"M-S99-2\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log should reference M-S99-2 root cause\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: autonomous_mode absent → 1 violation (missing_field; M-S99-2 referenced)\"\n\n# --- TC4: autonomous_mode invalid value → 1 violation (invalid_value) ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$EXEC_FILE\" <<'EOF'\n# Current Execution\n\n**autonomous_mode**: yes\n\n## S101 — row\nEOF\nrun_hook_default\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC4\"\nif ! grep -q \"invalid_value\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: log should mention invalid_value\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: autonomous_mode='yes' → 1 violation (invalid_value)\"\n\n# --- TC5: 0 session rows → 1 violation (missing_session_rows) ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$EXEC_FILE\" <<'EOF'\n# Current Execution\n\n**autonomous_mode**: true\n\n(no session rows — empty)\nEOF\nrun_hook_default\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC5\"\nif ! grep -q \"missing_session_rows\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: log should mention missing_session_rows\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: 0 session rows → 1 violation (missing_session_rows)\"\n\n# --- TC6: source != startup|resume|clear → no-op skip ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$EXEC_FILE\" <<'EOF'\n# Current Execution\n\n**autonomous_mode**: true\n\n## S101 — row\nEOF\n# Pass payload with non-matching source (e.g. PreCompact). Note: this requires node;\n# if node missing, verifier falls back to default startup → test becomes vacuous\n# but still safe (would just exit-0 for clean state).\nif command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  run_hook_with_payload '{\"source\":\"PreCompact\"}'\n  if ! grep -q \"skipped source=PreCompact\" \"$LOG\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL TC6: log should record skipped source=PreCompact\"\n    cat \"$LOG\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\n  echo \"PASS TC6: source=PreCompact → no-op skip line\"\nelse\n  echo \"SKIP TC6: node unavailable; cannot test source-filter branch\"\nfi\n\n# --- TC7: multiple violations → notification lists all ---\nclean_state\nprintf '# Empty header\\n' > \"$EXEC_FILE\"  # No autonomous_mode + 0 session rows\nrun_hook_default\nassert_violations_eq 2 \"TC7\"\nif [ ! -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC7: notification file should exist\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nNOTIF_VIOL_COUNT=$(grep -cE '^- (missing_file|missing_field|missing_session_rows|invalid_value)' \"$NOTIF_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nNOTIF_VIOL_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_VIOL_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$NOTIF_VIOL_COUNT\" -lt 2 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC7: notification should list ≥2 violation lines (got $NOTIF_VIOL_COUNT)\"\n  cat \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC7: 2 violations listed in notification\"\n\n# --- TC8: M-S99-2 empirical reproduction — file shape exactly like S99 truncation outcome ---\n# S99 archive collapsed current-execution.md from 2738 LOC to 158 LOC and dropped the\n# **autonomous_mode** line that lived between header and S49b row. Reproduce that exact\n# shape and confirm verifier flags it.\nclean_state\ncat > \"$EXEC_FILE\" <<'EOF'\n# Current Execution — Routing Source of Truth\n\n> This is THE single source of truth for \"where is work currently happening\".\n> Agent reads this at session start to identify active track.\n\n## S99 — Phase 3 — Tracking-bloat RCA Layer 1+2+3+5 IMPL ✅\n\n(content)\n\n## S98 — Phase 3 — sync-grilling-S98 ✅\n\n(content)\nEOF\nrun_hook_default\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC8\"\nif ! grep -q \"missing_field: \\*\\*autonomous_mode\\*\\*\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: M-S99-2 regression — should flag missing autonomous_mode field\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"M-S99-2\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: notification should reference M-S99-2 lineage\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC8: M-S99-2 file-shape regression caught (missing autonomous_mode flagged)\"\n\necho \"ALL PASS (8/8)\"\nexit 0\n","numLines":236,"startLine":1,"totalLines":236}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7bc8ee3f-1360-49cb-84d1-db0aafbc67bd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"66bb6b09-7793-4b3c-9dbe-7f8a3ff59fa7","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

do\n44\t        if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n45\t            b=$(wc -c < \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n46\t            t=$((b / 4))\n47\t            echo \"[tier1-bloat-check]   ${t} tok  $f\" >&2\n48\t        fi\n49\t    done\n50\t    echo \"[tier1-bloat-check] Action: re-shape heaviest file or move non-essential prose to Tier 2.\" >&2\n51\tfi\n52\t\n53\texit 0\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"a299e700-b8ed-41b6-a18f-1bcefea0dd99","timestamp":"2026-05-06T12:50:00.226Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\tier1-bloat-check.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# tier1-bloat-check.sh — Tier 1 memory bloat alarm\n# Companion hook to agent-workspace/constitution/memory-tiers.md (ratified S38, D-017)\n#\n# Per memory-tiers.md Tier 1 budget: ≤8K tokens (PLAN session bootstrap ceiling per\n# autonomous-protocol.md Rule 4). If Tier 1 exceeds 8K consistently, files must be\n# re-shaped or split (heaviest file → Tier 2 with explicit-load discipline).\n#\n# Soft-warns at SessionStart (non-blocking); does NOT halt execution.\n# Token=[REDACTED:credential]: bytes/4 (ASCII heuristic; conservative).\n\nset -uo pipefail\ncd \"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n\nCHECKPOINT_FILE=\"agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nCURRENT_EXEC=\"agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nROOT_CLAUDE=\"CLAUDE.md\"\nWORKSPACE_CLAUDE=\"agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md\"\n\nTIER1_FILES=(\"$ROOT_CLAUDE\" \"$WORKSPACE_CLAUDE\" \"$CURRENT_EXEC\")\n\n# Include checkpoint only if recent (≤24h)\nif [ -f \"$CHECKPOINT_FILE\" ]; then\n    if find \"$CHECKPOINT_FILE\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n        TIER1_FILES+=(\"$CHECKPOINT_FILE\")\n    fi\nfi\n\nTOTAL_BYTES=0\nfor f in \"${TIER1_FILES[@]}\"; do\n    if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n        b=$(wc -c < \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n        TOTAL_BYTES=$((TOTAL_BYTES + b))\n    fi\ndone\n\nTOTAL_TOKENS=$((TOTAL_BYTES / 4))\nCEILING=8000\n\nif [ \"$TOTAL_TOKENS\" -gt \"$CEILING\" ]; then\n    echo \"[tier1-bloat-check] WARN: Tier 1 bootstrap = ${TOTAL_TOKENS} tok (ceiling ${CEILING})\" >&2\n    echo \"[tier1-bloat-check] Files:\" >&2\n    for f in \"${TIER1_FILES[@]}\"; do\n        if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n            b=$(wc -c < \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n            t=$((b / 4))\n            echo \"[tier1-bloat-check]   ${t} tok  $f\" >&2\n        fi\n    done\n    echo \"[tier1-bloat-check] Action: re-shape heaviest file or move non-essential prose to Tier 2.\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":54,"startLine":1,"totalLines":54}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cbe8736f-01a4-45f7-8780-50f99cad41f7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"66bb6b09-7793-4b3c-9dbe-7f8a3ff59fa7","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"c0d4291c-bfcd-4da3-98ac-4d3674b570fb","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01ERtAPpNPcHQCL8oQVaneTi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ReqaC7GYLs9eBdEdFpimKG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\working-memory-budget-audit.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# working-memory-budget-audit.sh — SessionStart hook (preventive, S103 deliverable).\n#\n# Purpose: Enforce the \"routine load\" working-memory budget per S99 proposal\n# `agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md` § Tier 1 (≤ 20 KB combined). The three\n# files an agent loads on EVERY `continue` (boot-summary + latest checkpoint +\n# routing-config) MUST stay under the combined ceiling so /clear-resume context\n# remains compact. This is distinct from `tier1-bloat-check.sh` (broader CLAUDE.md +\n# current-execution.md set; older 8K-token ceiling from S38 D-017).\n#\n# Files audited (combined byte sum):\n#   1. agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md        (sub-cap ≤  4 KB recommended)\n#   2. agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md  (sub-cap ≤  8 KB recommended)\n#   3. agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md      (sub-cap ≤  8 KB recommended)\n#\n# Ceiling: 20480 bytes combined (= 20 KB hard byte ceiling per CLAUDE.md hard rule:\n#   \"Working-memory budget per agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md § Tier 1\n#    = ≤ 20 KB combined for routine load.\")\n#\n# Behavior on breach:\n#   - WARN to .session-hooks.log\n#   - Create dated notification at human-workspace/notifications/\n#   - Emit additionalContext (system-reminder-style) so agent surfaces issue at boot\n#   - exit 0 always (soft-warn; never HARD-BLOCK; harness fixing requires loading\n#     these very files, so blocking would lock-out the recovery path)\n#\n# Order: wired AFTER essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh BUT BEFORE\n# session-start-bootstrap.sh in .claude/settings.json — both pre-bootstrap concerns\n# (routing-fields presence + working-memory-budget shape) surface before bootstrap\n# silently parses the over-large files.\n#\n# Phase 0 portability per L-S11-1: bash + POSIX only (node optional for JSON emit;\n# audit still functions if node missing — just skips additionalContext output).\n# PIPEFAIL pattern per L-S48d-1 + L-S80-2: `2>/dev/null || true` after wc/grep.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nBOOT_SUMMARY=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nROUTING_CONFIG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nCEILING_BYTES=20480  # 20 KB combined budget\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$HOOK_LOG\")\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Read SessionStart payload to filter event source (only act on startup|resume|clear).\nPAYLOAD=\"\"\nif [ ! -t 0 ]; then\n  PAYLOAD=$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\nfi\nSOURCE=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  SOURCE=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\nlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\nprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).source||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nfi\n# Fallback: if no payload (e.g. firing-test direct invocation), default to startup so\n# audit still runs; production hook chain always has payload via Anthropic harness.\nSOURCE=\"${SOURCE:-startup}\"\n\ncase \"$SOURCE\" in\n  startup|resume|clear) ;;\n  *)\n    echo \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: skipped source=$SOURCE (not startup|resume|clear)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n    ;;\nesac\n\n# Sum bytes of routine-load files. Missing files contribute 0 bytes (the\n# essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh + bootstrap-summary-renderer.sh hooks own\n# the missing-file detection responsibility — this hook is only about budget).\nfile_bytes() {\n  local f=\"$1\"\n  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    local b\n    b=$(wc -c < \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    # wc -c may return whitespace-prefixed; strip via arithmetic coercion.\n    printf '%s' \"$((b + 0))\"\n  else\n    printf '0'\n  fi\n}\n\nBS_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\")\nCP_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$CHECKPOINT\")\nRC_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\")\nTOTAL_BYTES=$((BS_BYTES + CP_BYTES + RC_BYTES))\n\n# Track which files are missing (informational only; not a violation).\nMISSING_FILES=()\n[ ! -f \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\" ]    && MISSING_FILES+=(\"boot-summary.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]      && MISSING_FILES+=(\"checkpoints/latest.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\" ]  && MISSING_FILES+=(\"routing-config.md\")\n\nOVER_BUDGET=0\nif [ \"$TOTAL_BYTES\" -gt \"$CEILING_BYTES\" ]; then\n  OVER_BUDGET=1\nfi\n\necho \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: total=${TOTAL_BYTES}B ceiling=${CEILING_BYTES}B over_budget=${OVER_BUDGET} source=$SOURCE missing_count=${#MISSING_FILES[@]}\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$OVER_BUDGET\" -eq 1 ]; then\n  OVER_BY=$((TOTAL_BYTES - CEILING_BYTES))\n  PCT_OVER=$((OVER_BY * 100 / CEILING_BYTES))\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/working-memory-budget-OVER-$(date +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || echo unknown).md\"\n  {\n    echo \"# Working-memory budget exceeded — $TS\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"**Source**: $SOURCE\"\n    echo \"**Total**: ${TOTAL_BYTES} bytes\"\n    echo \"**Ceiling**: ${CEILING_BYTES} bytes (20 KB)\"\n    echo \"**Over by**: ${OVER_BY} bytes (${PCT_OVER}%)\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"## Per-file breakdown\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"| File | Bytes | Sub-cap (KB) |\"\n    echo \"|---|---|---|\"\n    echo \"| boot-summary.md     | ${BS_BYTES} | ≤ 4 |\"\n    echo \"| checkpoints/latest.md | ${CP_BYTES} | ≤ 8 |\"\n    echo \"| routing-config.md   | ${RC_BYTES} | ≤ 8 |\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"## Action required\"\n    echo \"- Identify heaviest file and re-shape:\"\n    echo \"  - boot-summary.md → re-render via bootstrap-summary-renderer.sh\"\n    echo \"  - checkpoints/latest.md → trim non-essential prose; archive bulk to dated checkpoint\"\n    echo \"  - routing-config.md → split task-class entries to extension file\"\n    echo \"- Reference: \\`agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md\\` § Tier 1\"\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n\n  # Emit additionalContext to surface to agent at session boot.\n  if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    CTX=\"WORKING-MEMORY BUDGET EXCEEDED — routine-load files total ${TOTAL_BYTES}B / ${CEILING_BYTES}B ceiling (over by ${OVER_BY}B / ${PCT_OVER}%). See $NOTIF_FILE. Trim heaviest of: boot-summary / latest checkpoint / routing-config.\"\n    node -e \"\nconst ctx = process.argv[1];\nprocess.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({\n  hookSpecificOutput: {\n    hookEventName: 'SessionStart',\n    additionalContext: ctx\n  }\n}));\n\" \"$CTX\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1684,"cache_read_input_tokens":104817,"output_tokens":3769,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1684,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3769,"cache_read_input_tokens":104817,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1684,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1684},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CamLydebw79VUaPpyujSa","type":"assistant","uuid":"b5caa84b-e073-4055-a633-fc6366377e1a","timestamp":"2026-05-06T12:51:06.852Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"66bb6b09-7793-4b3c-9dbe-7f8a3ff59fa7","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

The three\n# files an agent loads on EVERY `continue` (boot-summary + latest checkpoint +\n# routing-config) MUST stay under the combined ceiling so /clear-resume context\n# remains compact. This is distinct from `tier1-bloat-check.sh` (broader CLAUDE.md +\n# current-execution.md set; older 8K-token ceiling from S38 D-017).\n#\n# Files audited (combined byte sum):\n#   1. agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md        (sub-cap ≤  4 KB recommended)\n#   2. agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md  (sub-cap ≤  8 KB recommended)\n#   3. agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md      (sub-cap ≤  8 KB recommended)\n#\n# Ceiling: 20480 bytes combined (= 20 KB hard byte ceiling per CLAUDE.md hard rule:\n#   \"Working-memory budget per agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md § Tier 1\n#    = ≤ 20 KB combined for routine load.\")\n#\n# Behavior on breach:\n#   - WARN to .session-hooks.log\n#   - Create dated notification at human-workspace/notifications/\n#   - Emit additionalContext (system-reminder-style) so agent surfaces issue at boot\n#   - exit 0 always (soft-warn; never HARD-BLOCK; harness fixing requires loading\n#     these very files, so blocking would lock-out the recovery path)\n#\n# Order: wired AFTER essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh BUT BEFORE\n# session-start-bootstrap.sh in .claude/settings.json — both pre-bootstrap concerns\n# (routing-fields presence + working-memory-budget shape) surface before bootstrap\n# silently parses the over-large files.\n#\n# Phase 0 portability per L-S11-1: bash + POSIX only (node optional for JSON emit;\n# audit still functions if node missing — just skips additionalContext output).\n# PIPEFAIL pattern per L-S48d-1 + L-S80-2: `2>/dev/null || true` after wc/grep.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nBOOT_SUMMARY=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nROUTING_CONFIG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nCEILING_BYTES=20480  # 20 KB combined budget\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$HOOK_LOG\")\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Read SessionStart payload to filter event source (only act on startup|resume|clear).\nPAYLOAD=\"\"\nif [ ! -t 0 ]; then\n  PAYLOAD=$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\nfi\nSOURCE=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  SOURCE=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\nlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\nprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).source||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nfi\n# Fallback: if no payload (e.g. firing-test direct invocation), default to startup so\n# audit still runs; production hook chain always has payload via Anthropic harness.\nSOURCE=\"${SOURCE:-startup}\"\n\ncase \"$SOURCE\" in\n  startup|resume|clear) ;;\n  *)\n    echo \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: skipped source=$SOURCE (not startup|resume|clear)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n    ;;\nesac\n\n# Sum bytes of routine-load files. Missing files contribute 0 bytes (the\n# essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh + bootstrap-summary-renderer.sh hooks own\n# the missing-file detection responsibility — this hook is only about budget).\nfile_bytes() {\n  local f=\"$1\"\n  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    local b\n    b=$(wc -c < \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    # wc -c may return whitespace-prefixed; strip via arithmetic coercion.\n    printf '%s' \"$((b + 0))\"\n  else\n    printf '0'\n  fi\n}\n\nBS_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\")\nCP_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$CHECKPOINT\")\nRC_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\")\nTOTAL_BYTES=$((BS_BYTES + CP_BYTES + RC_BYTES))\n\n# Track which files are missing (informational only; not a violation).\nMISSING_FILES=()\n[ ! -f \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\" ]    && MISSING_FILES+=(\"boot-summary.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]      && MISSING_FILES+=(\"checkpoints/latest.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\" ]  && MISSING_FILES+=(\"routing-config.md\")\n\nOVER_BUDGET=0\nif [ \"$TOTAL_BYTES\" -gt \"$CEILING_BYTES\" ]; then\n  OVER_BUDGET=1\nfi\n\necho \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: total=${TOTAL_BYTES}B ceiling=${CEILING_BYTES}B over_budget=${OVER_BUDGET} source=$SOURCE missing_count=${#MISSING_FILES[@]}\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$OVER_BUDGET\" -eq 1 ]; then\n  OVER_BY=$((TOTAL_BYTES - CEILING_BYTES))\n  PCT_OVER=$((OVER_BY * 100 / CEILING_BYTES))\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/working-memory-budget-OVER-$(date +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || echo unknown).md\"\n  {\n    echo \"# Working-memory budget exceeded — $TS\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"**Source**: $SOURCE\"\n    echo \"**Total**: ${TOTAL_BYTES} bytes\"\n    echo \"**Ceiling**: ${CEILING_BYTES} bytes (20 KB)\"\n    echo \"**Over by**: ${OVER_BY} bytes (${PCT_OVER}%)\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"## Per-file breakdown\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"| File | Bytes | Sub-cap (KB) |\"\n    echo \"|---|---|---|\"\n    echo \"| boot-summary.md     | ${BS_BYTES} | ≤ 4 |\"\n    echo \"| checkpoints/latest.md | ${CP_BYTES} | ≤ 8 |\"\n    echo \"| routing-config.md   | ${RC_BYTES} | ≤ 8 |\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"## Action required\"\n    echo \"- Identify heaviest file and re-shape:\"\n    echo \"  - boot-summary.md → re-render via bootstrap-summary-renderer.sh\"\n    echo \"  - checkpoints/latest.md → trim non-essential prose; archive bulk to dated checkpoint\"\n    echo \"  - routing-config.md → split task-class entries to extension file\"\n    echo \"- Reference: \\`agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md\\` § Tier 1\"\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n\n  # Emit additionalContext to surface to agent at session boot.\n  if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    CTX=\"WORKING-MEMORY BUDGET EXCEEDED — routine-load files total ${TOTAL_BYTES}B / ${CEILING_BYTES}B ceiling (over by ${OVER_BY}B / ${PCT_OVER}%). See $NOTIF_FILE. Trim heaviest of: boot-summary / latest checkpoint / routing-config.\"\n    node -e \"\nconst ctx = process.argv[1];\nprocess.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({\n  hookSpecificOutput: {\n    hookEventName: 'SessionStart',\n    additionalContext: ctx\n  }\n}));\n\" \"$CTX\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b5caa84b-e073-4055-a633-fc6366377e1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"66bb6b09-7793-4b3c-9dbe-7f8a3ff59fa7","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"8b7ef375-95f8-4225-9bbe-05626f1bf58a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01GsRqySb2JDSmGpK9ZwdkJv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013ohk6FtQGfZTxD231d412P","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\working-memory-budget-audit-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for working-memory-budget-audit.sh (S103 deliverable per Phase 3.5\n# T7 hard rule #2 — every hook ships with companion firing-test).\n#\n# Hook purpose (SessionStart): audit combined byte size of the 3 \"routine load\"\n# files (boot-summary.md + checkpoints/latest.md + routing-config.md). Ceiling\n# 20480 bytes (20 KB) per CLAUDE.md hard rule + memory-tiers.md proposal § Tier 1.\n#\n# Behavior on breach: WARN to log + notification + additionalContext. Always exit 0.\n#\n# Test cases (8 TC):\n#   TC1 — clean state under budget (15 KB total) → over_budget=0, no notification\n#   TC2 — breach: total > 20480 (e.g. 22 KB) → over_budget=1, notification + ctx\n#   TC3 — boundary EXACTLY 20480 → over_budget=0 (strict greater-than)\n#   TC4 — boundary 20481 (1 byte over) → over_budget=1\n#   TC5 — boot-summary missing but other two under cap → 0 violations; missing tally =1\n#   TC6 — all 3 files missing → total=0 → over_budget=0 (no false alarm; missing-file\n#         detection is essential-routing-fields-verifier's domain not this hook's)\n#   TC7 — source=PreCompact → exit 0 no-op skip line in log\n#   TC8 — single oversized file (25 KB checkpoint alone) → over_budget=1; notification\n#         per-file breakdown lists exact byte counts\n#\n# Exit 0 = all pass. Exit 1 = any fail.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../working-memory-budget-audit.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap '[ -n \"${KEEP_TEMP:-}\" ] && echo \"(KEEP_TEMP set; tempdir at $TEMPDIR)\" || rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nBOOT_SUMMARY=\"$MEM_DIR/boot-summary.md\"\nCHECKPOINT_DIR=\"$MEM_DIR/checkpoints\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$CHECKPOINT_DIR/latest.md\"\nROUTING_CONFIG=\"$MEM_DIR/routing-config.md\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/working-memory-budget-OVER-$(date +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || echo unknown).md\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\" \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n}\n\n# Generate a file of approximately N bytes (deterministic, no randomness).\nmake_bytes() {\n  local target=\"$1\" out=\"$2\"\n  local n=$((target / 64))   # 64-byte block × n\n  local rem=$((target % 64))\n  printf '' > \"$out\"\n  local i=0\n  while [ $i -lt $n ]; do\n    printf 'AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAABBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBB' >> \"$out\"\n    i=$((i + 1))\n  done\n  if [ \"$rem\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    printf '%*s' \"$rem\" '' | tr ' ' 'X' >> \"$out\"\n  fi\n}\n\nrun_hook_with_payload() {\n  local payload=\"$1\"\n  printf '%s' \"$payload\" | CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\nrun_hook_default() {\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\nassert_log_contains() {\n  local pat=\"$1\" tag=\"$2\"\n  if ! grep -q \"$pat\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    echo \"FAIL $tag: log missing pattern: $pat\"\n    [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && cat \"$LOG\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\n}\n\nlast_log_field() {\n  local key=\"$1\"\n  grep \"working-memory-budget-audit:\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | grep -oE \"${key}=[0-9]+\" | sed \"s/${key}=//\" || true\n}\n\n# --- TC1: clean state under budget (≈15 KB total) → over_budget=0 ---\nclean_state\nmake_bytes 1500  \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\"     # 1.5 KB\nmake_bytes 7000  \"$CHECKPOINT\"       # 7 KB\nmake_bytes 6500  \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\"   # 6.5 KB → total 15 KB\nrun_hook_default\nTOTAL=$(last_log_field total)\nTOTAL_NUM=$(printf '%s' \"$TOTAL\" | tr -d 'B')\nOVER=$(last_log_field over_budget)\nif [ \"${TOTAL_NUM:-0}\" -ne 15000 ]; -f \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\" ]    && MISSING_FILES+=(\"boot-summary.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]      && MISSING_FILES+=(\"checkpoints/latest.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\" ]  && MISSING_FILES+=(\"routing-config.md\")\n\nOVER_BUDGET=0\nif [ \"$TOTAL_BYTES\" -gt \"$CEILING_BYTES\" ]; then\n  OVER_BUDGET=1\nfi\n\necho \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: total=${TOTAL_BYTES}B ceiling=${CEILING_BYTES}B over_budget=${OVER_BUDGET} source=$SOURCE missing_count=${#MISSING_FILES[@]}\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"","newString":"# Track how many of the 3 routine-load files are missing (informational only; not a\n# violation — missing-file detection is essential-routing-fields-verifier's domain).\nMISSING_COUNT=0\n[ ! -f \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\" ]   && MISSING_COUNT=$((MISSING_COUNT + 1))\n[ ! -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]     && MISSING_COUNT=$((MISSING_COUNT + 1))\n[ ! -f \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\" ] && MISSING_COUNT=$((MISSING_COUNT + 1))\n\nOVER_BUDGET=0\nif [ \"$TOTAL_BYTES\" -gt \"$CEILING_BYTES\" ]; then\n  OVER_BUDGET=1\nfi\n\necho \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: total=${TOTAL_BYTES}B ceiling=${CEILING_BYTES}B over_budget=${OVER_BUDGET} source=$SOURCE missing_count=${MISSING_COUNT}\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# working-memory-budget-audit.sh — SessionStart hook (preventive, S103 deliverable).\n#\n# Purpose: Enforce the \"routine load\" working-memory budget per S99 proposal\n# `agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md` § Tier 1 (≤ 20 KB combined). The three\n# files an agent loads on EVERY `continue` (boot-summary + latest checkpoint +\n# routing-config) MUST stay under the combined ceiling so /clear-resume context\n# remains compact. This is distinct from `tier1-bloat-check.sh` (broader CLAUDE.md +\n# current-execution.md set; older 8K-token ceiling from S38 D-017).\n#\n# Files audited (combined byte sum):\n#   1. agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md        (sub-cap ≤  4 KB recommended)\n#   2. agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md  (sub-cap ≤  8 KB recommended)\n#   3. agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md      (sub-cap ≤  8 KB recommended)\n#\n# Ceiling: 20480 bytes combined (= 20 KB hard byte ceiling per CLAUDE.md hard rule:\n#   \"Working-memory budget per agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md § Tier 1\n#    = ≤ 20 KB combined for routine load.\")\n#\n# Behavior on breach:\n#   - WARN to .session-hooks.log\n#   - Create dated notification at human-workspace/notifications/\n#   - Emit additionalContext (system-reminder-style) so agent surfaces issue at boot\n#   - exit 0 always (soft-warn; never HARD-BLOCK; harness fixing requires loading\n#     these very files, so blocking would lock-out the recovery path)\n#\n# Order: wired AFTER essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh BUT BEFORE\n# session-start-bootstrap.sh in .claude/settings.json — both pre-bootstrap concerns\n# (routing-fields presence + working-memory-budget shape) surface before bootstrap\n# silently parses the over-large files.\n#\n# Phase 0 portability per L-S11-1: bash + POSIX only (node optional for JSON emit;\n# audit still functions if node missing — just skips additionalContext output).\n# PIPEFAIL pattern per L-S48d-1 + L-S80-2: `2>/dev/null || true` after wc/grep.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nBOOT_SUMMARY=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/boot-summary.md\"\nCHECKPOINT=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nROUTING_CONFIG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nCEILING_BYTES=20480  # 20 KB combined budget\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$HOOK_LOG\")\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\n# Read SessionStart payload to filter event source (only act on startup|resume|clear).\nPAYLOAD=\"\"\nif [ ! -t 0 ]; then\n  PAYLOAD=$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\nfi\nSOURCE=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  SOURCE=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\nlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\nprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).source||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nfi\n# Fallback: if no payload (e.g. firing-test direct invocation), default to startup so\n# audit still runs; production hook chain always has payload via Anthropic harness.\nSOURCE=\"${SOURCE:-startup}\"\n\ncase \"$SOURCE\" in\n  startup|resume|clear) ;;\n  *)\n    echo \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: skipped source=$SOURCE (not startup|resume|clear)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n    ;;\nesac\n\n# Sum bytes of routine-load files. Missing files contribute 0 bytes (the\n# essential-routing-fields-verifier.sh + bootstrap-summary-renderer.sh hooks own\n# the missing-file detection responsibility — this hook is only about budget).\nfile_bytes() {\n  local f=\"$1\"\n  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    local b\n    b=$(wc -c < \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    # wc -c may return whitespace-prefixed; strip via arithmetic coercion.\n    printf '%s' \"$((b + 0))\"\n  else\n    printf '0'\n  fi\n}\n\nBS_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\")\nCP_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$CHECKPOINT\")\nRC_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\")\nTOTAL_BYTES=$((BS_BYTES + CP_BYTES + RC_BYTES))\n\n# Track which files are missing (informational only; not a violation).\nMISSING_FILES=()\n[ ! -f \"$BOOT_SUMMARY\" ]    && MISSING_FILES+=(\"boot-summary.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$CHECKPOINT\" ]      && MISSING_FILES+=(\"checkpoints/latest.md\")\n[ ! -f \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\" ]  && MISSING_FILES+=(\"routing-config.md\")\n\nOVER_BUDGET=0\nif [ \"$TOTAL_BYTES\" -gt \"$CEILING_BYTES\" ]; then\n  OVER_BUDGET=1\nfi\n\necho \"[$TS] working-memory-budget-audit: total=${TOTAL_BYTES}B ceiling=${CEILING_BYTES}B over_budget=${OVER_BUDGET} source=$SOURCE missing_count=${#MISSING_FILES[@]}\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nif [ \"$OVER_BUDGET\" -eq 1 ]; then\n  OVER_BY=$((TOTAL_BYTES - CEILING_BYTES))\n  PCT_OVER=$((OVER_BY * 100 / CEILING_BYTES))\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/working-memory-budget-OVER-$(date +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || echo unknown).md\"\n  {\n    echo \"# Working-memory budget exceeded — $TS\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"**Source**: $SOURCE\"\n    echo \"**Total**: ${TOTAL_BYTES} bytes\"\n    echo \"**Ceiling**: ${CEILING_BYTES} bytes (20 KB)\"\n    echo \"**Over by**: ${OVER_BY} bytes (${PCT_OVER}%)\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"## Per-file breakdown\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"| File | Bytes | Sub-cap (KB) |\"\n    echo \"|---|---|---|\"\n    echo \"| boot-summary.md     | ${BS_BYTES} | ≤ 4 |\"\n    echo \"| checkpoints/latest.md | ${CP_BYTES} | ≤ 8 |\"\n    echo \"| routing-config.md   | ${RC_BYTES} | ≤ 8 |\"\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"## Action required\"\n    echo \"- Identify heaviest file and re-shape:\"\n    echo \"  - boot-summary.md → re-render via bootstrap-summary-renderer.sh\"\n    echo \"  - checkpoints/latest.md → trim non-essential prose; archive bulk to dated checkpoint\"\n    echo \"  - routing-config.md → split task-class entries to extension file\"\n    echo \"- Reference: \\`agent-workspace/proposals/memory-tiers.md\\` § Tier 1\"\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n\n  # Emit additionalContext to surface to agent at session boot.\n  if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    CTX=\"WORKING-MEMORY BUDGET EXCEEDED — routine-load files total ${TOTAL_BYTES}B / ${CEILING_BYTES}B ceiling (over by ${OVER_BY}B / ${PCT_OVER}%). See $NOTIF_FILE. Trim heaviest of: boot-summary / latest checkpoint / routing-config.\"\n    node -e \"\nconst ctx = process.argv[1];\nprocess.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({\n  hookSpecificOutput: {\n    hookEventName: 'SessionStart',\n    additionalContext: ctx\n  }\n}));\n\" \"$CTX\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":91,"oldLines":18,"newStart":91,"newLines":19,"lines":[" RC_BYTES=$(file_bytes \"$ROUTING_CONFIG\")"," TOTAL_BYTES=$((BS_BYTES + CP_BYTES + RC_BYTES))"," ","-# Track which files are missing (informational only; not a violation).","-MISSING_FILES=()","-[ ! This wrapper adds defense-in-depth by\n# hiding the $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA optional positional arg, eliminating the arg-position\n# confusion that caused both M-S98-1 and M-S101-1 (long reason text or decision_id\n# landing in $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA slot, awk replay coercing non-numeric to 0).\n#\n# Contract (5 positional args, all required):\n#   $1 category         e.g. SCOPE | LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | DECISION_ROUTING\n#   $2 event_type       e.g. charter_match | drift_signal | q_and_a_resolution | decision_correctness\n#   $3 decision_id      e.g. sync-grilling-S102 (no spaces; identifies the event)\n#   $4 source_evidence  file path to source (e.g. agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md)\n#   $5 reason           free-form text describing why this event was recorded\n#\n# Behavior: always passes \"\" (empty) as $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA to sync-tracker-update.sh,\n# triggering the weights.yaml `weight_<event_type>` lookup. Override mode is unsupported\n# in this wrapper (use sync-tracker-update.sh directly for non-default deltas).\n#\n# Usage:\n#   sync-grilling-call.sh SCOPE charter_match sync-grilling-S102 \\\n#     agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md \\\n#     \"S102 sync-grilling refresher: routine auto-tier; ...\"\n#\n# Phase 0 portability per L-S11-1: bash + POSIX only.\n# Per L-S65-2 (harness priority #1): this is a routine convenience layer, NOT a\n# replacement for the deterministic guard already in sync-tracker-update.sh.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nCAT=\"${1:-}\"\nEVT=\"${2:-}\"\nDEC_ID=\"${3:-}\"\nSRC_EV=\"${4:-}\"\nREASON=\"${5:-}\"\n\nif [ -z \"$CAT\" ] || [ -z \"$EVT\" ] || [ -z \"$DEC_ID\" ] || [ -z \"$SRC_EV\" ] || [ -z \"$REASON\" ]; then\n  cat >&2 <<USAGE\n[sync-grilling-call] usage: $0 <category> <event_type> <decision_id> <source_evidence> <reason>\n\nAll 5 args required. Example:\n  $0 SCOPE charter_match sync-grilling-S102 \\\\\n     agent-workspace/memory/sync-state.md \\\\\n     \"S102 sync-grilling refresher: routine auto-tier; ...\"\n\nCategories: LANGUAGE | DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS | DESIGN_THINKING | SCOPE | DECISION_ROUTING\nEvent types: decision_correctness | charter_match | q_and_a_resolution\n             drift_signal | decision_correction | decision_revocation | thesis_revocation\n\nPer M-S98-1 + M-S101-1 prevention: this wrapper hides the \\$3 OVERRIDE_DELTA arg to\nprevent the arg-position confusion that caused both mistakes. For non-default deltas,\ncall sync-tracker-update.sh directly.\nUSAGE\n  exit 2\nfi\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" && pwd)\"\nUPDATE_HOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/sync-tracker-update.sh\"\nif [ ! -f \"$UPDATE_HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"[sync-grilling-call] sync-tracker-update.sh not found at $UPDATE_HOOK\" >&2\n  exit 2\nfi\n\n# Always pass \"\" as $3 OVERRIDE_DELTA → underlying script uses weight_<event_type> from weights.yaml.\nexec bash \"$UPDATE_HOOK\" \"$CAT\" \"$EVT\" \"\" \"$DEC_ID\" \"$SRC_EV\" \"$REASON\"\n","numLines":67,"startLine":1,"totalLines":67}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"12668b6e-ca69-42b6-b378-a6761fcec90a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"66bb6b09-7793-4b3c-9dbe-7f8a3ff59fa7","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
